{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01G5oPQrMRPN84nvHZEsETyZ","type":"tool_result","content":"296\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n297\t\n298\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n299\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n300\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n301\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n302\t        raise GovernanceError(\n303\t            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n304\t            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n305\t        )\n306\t\n307\t    # Typed-judgment plan — a typed block folds through the typed parser:\n308\t    # the verdict is its value, the confidence its top-label probability,\n309\t    # the refs the request's own strings materialized from its indices.\n310\t    confidence_source, _source_error = stamped_confidence_source(details)\n311\t    typed = typed_verdict_block(verdict_block)\n312\t    typed_answer: TypedAnswer | None = None\n313\t    if typed is not None:\n314\t        question, outcome = parse_typed_verdict(request, typed)\n315\t        if isinstance(outcome, ParseFailure):\n316\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"judge bridge typed verdict refused: {outcome.reason_code}: {outcome.detail}\")\n317\t        typed_answer = outcome\n318\t        verdict_block = {\n319\t            **verdict_block,\n320\t            \"verdict\": typed_answer.value,\n321\t            \"confidence\": typed_answer.confidence,\n322\t            \"rationale\": typed_answer.rationale,\n323\t            \"evidence_refs\": materialize_evidence_refs(question, typed_answer),\n324\t        }\n325\t\n326\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n327\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n328\t        raise GovernanceError(\n329\t            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n330\t        )\n331\t    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n332\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n333\t    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n334\t    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n335\t    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n336\t    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n337\t    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n338\t    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n339\t    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n340\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n341\t    if not judge_id:\n342\t        raise GovernanceError(\n343\t            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n344\t            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n345\t        )\n346\t    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n347\t        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n348\t        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n349\t        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n350\t        raise GovernanceError(\n351\t            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n352\t        )\n353\t\n354\t    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n355\t    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n356\t    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n357\t    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n358\t\n359\t    note = (\n360\t        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n361\t        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n362\t        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n363\t        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n364\t    )\n365\t    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n366\t        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n367\t\n368\t    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n369\t    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n370\t\n371\t    row = record_operator_feedback(\n372\t        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n373\t        run_id=str(run_id),\n374\t        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n375\t        verdict=verdict,\n376\t        severity=severity,\n377\t        note=note,\n378\t        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n379\t        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n380\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n381\t        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n382\t        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n383\t        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n384\t        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n385\t        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n386\t        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n387\t        # predate the stamp.\n388\t        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n389\t        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n390\t        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n391\t        rationale=str(note),\n392\t        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n393\t        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n394\t        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n395\t        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n396\t        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n397\t        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n398\t        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n399\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n400\t        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n401\t        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n402\t        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n403\t        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n404\t        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n405\t        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n406\t        confidence_source=confidence_source if confidence is not None else None,\n407\t        evidence_selection=\"index\" if typed_answer is not None else \"ref\",\n408\t        base_dir=base_dir,\n409\t    )\n410\t    return row"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"66962382-f920-48a2-964d-9f75acea2f64","uuid":"36792ce7-93f6-4b39-9d09-e94bff885dde","timestamp":"2026-09-21T01:35:11.880Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-214613d2c9c1/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judgment_bridge.py","content":"    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # Typed-judgment plan — a typed block folds through the typed parser:\n    # the verdict is its value, the confidence its top-label probability,\n    # the refs the request's own strings materialized from its indices.\n    confidence_source, _source_error = stamped_confidence_source(details)\n    typed = typed_verdict_block(verdict_block)\n    typed_answer: TypedAnswer | None = None\n    if typed is not None:\n        question, outcome = parse_typed_verdict(request, typed)\n        if isinstance(outcome, ParseFailure):\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"judge bridge typed verdict refused: {outcome.reason_code}: {outcome.detail}\")\n        typed_answer = outcome\n        verdict_block = {\n            **verdict_block,\n            \"verdict\": typed_answer.value,\n            \"confidence\": typed_answer.confidence,\n            \"rationale\": typed_answer.rationale,\n            \"evidence_refs\": materialize_evidence_refs(question, typed_answer),\n        }\n\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n        )\n    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n        )\n    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n\n    note = (\n        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n    )\n    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n\n    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n\n    row = record_operator_feedback(\n        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n        run_id=str(run_id),\n        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n        verdict=verdict,\n        severity=severity,\n        note=note,\n        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n        # predate the stamp.\n        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n        rationale=str(note),\n        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n        confidence_source=confidence_source if confidence is not None else None,\n        evidence_selection=\"index\" if typed_answer is not None else \"ref\",\n        base_dir=base_dir,\n    )\n    return row","numLines":115,"startLine":296,"totalLines":499}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfFj42yiBJgwzCkEx6RQK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"**Verdict: false_positive** — the flagged raw query is the atomic failed-login UPDATE in `handleFailedLogin`, keyed on the user row's UUID primary key in a pre-authentication path where no caller tenant exists; `auth.users` is the platform's identity primitive that is deliberately excluded from tenant isolation, and the fix the rule implies would break lockout for SUPER_ADMIN rows (`tenantId IS NULL`).\n\nExcerpt note: the pinned excerpt (lines 1-206) did not reach the flagged region, so I read the file directly. At this commit the `this.dataSource.query(` call sits at line 1725; the finding's `:1708` is the scan-time line of the same call (the adapter anchors on `node.getStart()`, and the file contains exactly one `.query(` call).\n\n```json\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n  \"request_id\": \"AIR-aria-evidence-judge-214613d2c9c1\",\n  \"claim_id\": \"AIR-aria-evidence-judge-214613d2c9c1\",\n  \"agent_id\": \"aria-evidence-judge\",\n  \"role\": \"evidence_judgment\",\n  \"status\": \"submitted\",\n  \"satisfaction_matrix\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"verdict\",\n      \"verdict\": \"contradicted\",\n      \"note\": \"false_positive. The flagged raw query is the UPDATE auth.users statement in handleFailedLogin (this.dataSource.query at authentication.service.ts:1725; the finding's :1708 is the scan-time line of this same call, the only .query( call in the file). It is keyed on the loaded user's uuid primary key (WHERE id = $1 at :1738, bound to user.id at :1740; PK declared at user.entity.ts:84), it runs pre-authentication on password mismatch (:645-:647) after a lookup by globally unique email only (:534, :536; UNIQUE LOWER(email) index at migration 1800300000000 line 100), so there is no caller tenant to scope against and no other tenant's row the PK predicate can reach. auth.users is the identity primitive the RLS helper auto-skips (apply-tenant-rls.helper.ts:145) because login reads it before a tenant is known. tenantId is nullable (user.entity.ts:120) and SUPER_ADMIN rows carry NULL, so adding the tenant predicate the rule implies would match zero rows for platform admins, updatedAttempts would read 0 and lockout would never fire: a security regression. The adapter matched only because the audit-log payload at :1764 spells tenantId (scopeHasTenant regex, tenant-scoping-adapter.ts:232) while the SQL carries none (callHasTenantPredicate, :231); its gold true-positive fixture (raw-query.service.ts:7) is a SELECT by caller-supplied id inside a method that receives a tenantId parameter, a structurally different IDOR shape.\",\n \"aria-evidence-judge\",\n      \"model\": \"claude-opus-5\",\n      \"prompt_hash\": null,\n      \"confidence\": 0.92,\n      \"rationale\": \"The rule tenant_raw_query_missing_tenant_predicate exists to catch a raw SQL statement on a tenant-owned table that can read or write a row belonging to a tenant other than the caller's. The flagged statement is the atomic failed-login UPDATE in handleFailedLogin (authentication.service.ts:1712-1741; the call starts at :1725 at this commit, :1708 at scan time). Four facts remove the cross-tenant shape. (1) Path: it runs pre-authentication, invoked only on password mismatch (:645, :647), so the request carries no authenticated tenant and there is nothing to scope against; the enclosing login lookup is by email only by design (:534, :536). (2) Predicate: the UPDATE is keyed on the loaded row's uuid primary key (WHERE id = $1 at :1738, parameter user.id at :1740, PK at user.entity.ts:84) and email is globally unique (UNIQUE LOWER(email), migration 1800300000000:100), so exactly one row is reachable and a tenant predicate could not narrow it further. (3) Table contract: auth.users (user.entity.ts:64) is the identity primitive the RLS helper auto-skips via DEFAULT_IDENTITY_TABLES (apply-tenant-rls.helper.ts:145, applied at :339) because login must read it before a tenant is known. (4) The implied fix is harmful: tenantId is nullable (user.entity.ts:120) and SUPER_ADMIN rows hold NULL, so AND \\\"tenantId\\\" = $4 would match zero rows for platform admins, result[0] would be undefined, updatedAttempts would read 0 and the ACCOUNT_LOCKED path (:1753-:1759) would never fire for the most privileged accounts. Why the adapter fired: scopeHasTenant (tenant-scoping-adapter.ts:232) matched the token tenantId in the audit payload at :1764, callHasTenantPredicate (:231) found no tenant token in the SQL, and the gate at :259-:260 let the raw_query branch (:265) emit. Its gold true-positive (fixtures raw-query.service.ts:7-8) is a SELECT by a caller-supplied id inside a method that RECEIVES a tenantId, the IDOR shape; a PK-keyed self-row write in a pre-auth identity flow is not that shape. If the operator wants this signal silenced at the root, allowlist the file in the adapter input or teach analyzeTenantUnit to treat a WHERE id = $n predicate bound to a loaded entity's PK in an identity-table method as predicated; do not add a NULL-hostile tenant predicate to the SQL.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n
